A Treatise on the Venereal Disease
John Hunter (1786)
========================================================================

PROVENANCE NOTE (Tower of Babel staging): Hunter's 1786 treatise on venereal disease — the first systematic clinical and experimental study of syphilis and gonorrhoea, including his famous (and self-disastrous) self-inoculation experiment. First edition. This edition is in the public domain.

========================================================================
